1. Community-small 

export HOGDIFF=/home/quang/HOG-Diff
export HOGDIFF_PYTHON=/home/quang/miniconda3/envs/hogdiff/bin/python

N=1024
DEVICE="gpu"

for SEED in {42..44}; do
  echo "========================================"
  echo "Running HoG-Diff community_small for SEED=$SEED"
  echo "========================================"

  RUN="seed_${SEED}"

  # Train
  PYTHONHASHSEED="$SEED" PYTHONPATH=src \
  python scripts/run_hog_diff_baseline.py \
    --stage train \
    --dataset community_small \
    --common-config configs/baselines/common_community_small.yaml \
    --wrapper-config configs/baselines/hog_diff_community_small.yaml \
    --seed-id "$SEED" \
    --overwrite \
    --run-id "$RUN" \
    --device "$DEVICE" || break

  # Generate
  PYTHONHASHSEED="$SEED" PYTHONPATH=src \
  python scripts/run_hog_diff_baseline.py \
    --stage generate \
    --dataset community_small \
    --common-config configs/baselines/common_community_small.yaml \
    --wrapper-config configs/baselines/hog_diff_community_small.yaml \
    --seed-id "$SEED" \
    --run-id "$RUN" \
    --num-samples "$N" \
    --device "$DEVICE" || break

  # Evaluate
  GEN_DIR="outputs/baselines/hog_diff/community_small/$RUN/generations/seed_${SEED}_n_${N}"

  PYTHONHASHSEED="$SEED" PYTHONPATH=src \
  python scripts/evaluate_graph_generation_report.py \
    --config configs/experiments/baselines/community_small_evaluation.yaml \
    --generated-dir "$GEN_DIR" \
    --output-dir "$GEN_DIR/evaluation_report" || break

  echo "Completed SEED=$SEED"
done

echo "HoG-Diff community_small loop finished."

2.  Ego-small

export HOGDIFF=/home/quang/HOG-Diff
export HOGDIFF_PYTHON=/home/quang/miniconda3/envs/hogdiff/bin/python

N=1024
DEVICE="gpu"

for SEED in {42..44}; do
  echo "========================================"
  echo "Running HoG-Diff ego_small for SEED=$SEED"
  echo "========================================"

  RUN="seed_${SEED}"

  # Train
  PYTHONHASHSEED="$SEED" PYTHONPATH=src \
  python scripts/run_hog_diff_baseline.py \
    --stage train \
    --dataset ego_small \
    --common-config configs/baselines/common_ego_small.yaml \
    --wrapper-config configs/baselines/hog_diff_ego_small.yaml \
    --seed-id "$SEED" \
    --overwrite \
    --run-id "$RUN" \
    --device "$DEVICE" || break

  # Generate
  PYTHONHASHSEED="$SEED" PYTHONPATH=src \
  python scripts/run_hog_diff_baseline.py \
    --stage generate \
    --dataset ego_small \
    --common-config configs/baselines/common_ego_small.yaml \
    --wrapper-config configs/baselines/hog_diff_ego_small.yaml \
    --seed-id "$SEED" \
    --run-id "$RUN" \
    --num-samples "$N" \
    --device "$DEVICE" || break

  # Evaluate
  GEN_DIR="outputs/baselines/hog_diff/ego_small/$RUN/generations/seed_${SEED}_n_${N}"

  PYTHONHASHSEED="$SEED" PYTHONPATH=src \
  python scripts/evaluate_graph_generation_report.py \
    --config configs/experiments/baselines/ego_small_evaluation.yaml \
    --generated-dir "$GEN_DIR" \
    --output-dir "$GEN_DIR/evaluation_report" || break

  echo "Completed SEED=$SEED"
done

echo "HoG-Diff ego_small loop finished."

3.  QM9
export HOGDIFF=/home/quang/HOG-Diff
export HOGDIFF_PYTHON=/home/quang/miniconda3/envs/hogdiff/bin/python

RUN=seed_42
N=10000

PYTHONPATH=src python scripts/run_hog_diff_baseline.py \
  --stage train \
  --dataset qm9 \
  --common-config configs/baselines/common_qm9.yaml \
  --wrapper-config configs/baselines/hog_diff_qm9.yaml \
  --seed-id 42 \
  --overwrite \
  --run-id "$RUN" \
  --device gpu

PYTHONPATH=src python scripts/run_hog_diff_baseline.py \
  --stage generate \
  --dataset qm9 \
  --common-config configs/baselines/common_qm9.yaml \
  --wrapper-config configs/baselines/hog_diff_qm9.yaml \
  --seed-id 42 \
  --overwrite \
  --run-id "$RUN" \
  --num-samples "$N" \
  --device gpu

GEN_DIR="outputs/baselines/hog_diff/qm9/$RUN/generations/seed_42_n_${N}"

PYTHONPATH=src python scripts/evaluate_generated_molecules.py \
  --generated-dir "$GEN_DIR" \
  --dataset-root outputs/datasets \
  --dataset qm9_attributed \
  --reference-split test \
  --train-split train \
  --require-fcd \
  --output-dir "$GEN_DIR/evaluation_molecules"

PYTHONHASHSEED=42 PYTHONPATH=src \
python scripts/evaluate_qed_research.py \
  --generated-graphs "$GEN_DIR/base_graphs.pkl" \
  --reference-graphs outputs/datasets/qm9_attributed/test.pkl \
  --molecular-report "$GEN_DIR/evaluation_molecules/molecular_evaluation_metrics.json" \
  --evaluator scripts/evaluate_generated_molecules.py \
  --expected-num-generated 10000 \
  --thresholds 0.5 0.7 \
  --output-dir "$GEN_DIR/evaluation_qed"
